# Model Inspection Cleanup Review Inventory

**Branch:** `refactor/model-inspection-cleanup`
**Base:** `a4138a613dd643abe12858eec5d1c3beb09e95e7`
**Phase 1 source evidence:** `401259594fe5bca365bb9bfd2e4619f41bfc574d` / permanent run `31234116989`
**Source list:** `docs/reviews/model-inspection-cleanup-source-files.txt`

This ledger records one review disposition for every file in the complete cleanup scope. Historical rows retain their Phase 1 evidence; later task rows record subsequent implementation without rewriting that history. The current source list and ledger contain 593 exact, sorted, unique paths. A `none in Phase 1` change means the file was inspected and deliberately left unchanged at that gate, not skipped. The local hosted-equivalent packaged Release/x64 candidate passed the exact two-category filtered suite 686/686 with zero non-passing results; its ephemeral raw TRX SHA-256 was `2FEDADEF0864218B055B3CF31E2D6D91839A1BE53D74EA778170F9BF137B2C41` and the raw file was not staged. Focused subsets passed the 15 new ordinary classes 286/286 and the real packaged N-001 page journey 1/1. Exact-floor Contracts passed 167/167 before the fixture-contract slice; the Task 2 report contracts passed 11/11, all fixture contracts passed 132/132, catalogue contracts passed 31/31, non-report fixture contracts passed 121/121, and the neutral Release fixture build passed with zero warnings/errors. Task 3 Debug/x64 packaged execution passed the three new fixture-runtime classes 59/59 and those classes plus the existing ViewModel suite passed 83/83; Task 3 cleanup reconciled at 577/577 on 2026-08-11, and Task 4 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 579/579 on 2026-08-12. Task 5 boundary contracts passed 53/53 and full Contracts passed 352/352; its fresh Release/x64 isolation run scanned 112 package files, proved the ReadyToRun main assembly and `resources.pri`, found zero forbidden fixture/gallery path, token or metadata hits, and published canonical evidence only after owned-root cleanup. Task 5 cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 581/581 on 2026-08-12. Task 6 registers twelve durable Debug/x64 fixture-gallery paths and cleanup reconciliation is recorded at 593/593 on 2026-08-12; focused hardening passed 13/13, the complete gallery passed 72/72, the integrated fixture/page suite passed 201/201, Release onboarding passed 29/29, boundary contracts passed 53/53, and full Contracts passed 352/352. Its Release graph contained 61 Compile items and three project references with zero Debug/fixture closure, while the fresh 513-file AppX scan found zero forbidden fixture/gallery path, ASCII or PRI hits. Hosted exact-head release evidence remains pending.
